 // Silme sonrası agent'a güncel LoRa config gönder\n2315\t    const edgeDevice = await this.deviceRepository.findOne({ where: { id: edgeDeviceId } });\n2316\t    if (edgeDevice) {\n2317\t      await removeLoRaDevice sonrası çağrılır —\n2326\t   * agent'ın LoRa cihaz listesini güncel tutmak için.\n2327\t   * Device offline ise sessizce atlanır (log ile bildirilir).\n2328\t   */\n2329\t  private async pushLoRaConfigToDevice(edgeDevice: EdgeDevice): Promise<void> {\n2330\t    if (!edgeDevice.isOnline) {\n2331\t      this.logger.debug(\n2332\t        `Skipping LoRa config push to ${edgeDevice.deviceCode} — device is offline`,\n2333\t      );\n2334\t      return;\n2335\t    }\n2336\t\n2337\t    let mqtt: MqttClientService;\n2338\t    try {\n2339\t      mqtt = = await this.buildLoRaWanConfig(edgeDevice.id);\n2346\t\n2347\t    // Cihaz listesi boşsa agent'a LoRa modülünü kapatması için boş liste gönder\n2348\t    const devices = loraConfig?.devices ?? [];\n2349\t\n2350\t    try {\n2351\t      await mqtt.publish(`tenants/${edgeDevice.tenantId}/devices/${edgeDevice.id}/commands`, {\n2352\t        commandId: randomUUID(),\n2353\t        command: 'update_lora_devices',\n2354\t        params: { devices },\n2355\t        timestamp: new Date(),\n2356\t      });\n2357\t      this.logger.log(`Pushed LoRa config to ${edgeDevice.deviceCode}: ${devices.length} devices`);\n2358\t    } catch (error) {\n2359\t      this.logger.error(\n2360\t        `Failed to push LoRa config to ${edgeDevice.deviceCode}: ${(error as Error).message}`,\n2361\t      );\n2362\t    }\n2363\t  }\n2364\t\n2365\t  /**\n2366\t   * Send a downlink payload to a LoRa end-device via MQTT.\n2367\t   *\n2368\t   * Edge agent, MQTT command topic'inden gelen downlink komutunu alır\n2369\t   * ve SX1302 üzerinden ilgili cihazın bir sonraki RX window'unda\n2370\t   * payload'ı iletir. LoRaActivationMode.OTAA,\n      deviceClass: input.deviceClass ?? LoRaDeviceClass.A,\n      codec: input.codec ?? 'cayenne_lpp',\n      adrEnabled: input.adrEnabled ?? true,\n      fPort: 1,\n      isJoined: false,\n    });\n\n    const saved = await this.loraDeviceRepository.save(device);\n    this.logger.log(\n      `Added LoRa device: ${saved.name} (DevEUI: ${saved.devEui}) to edge ${edgeDeviceId}`,\n    );\n\n    // Ekleme sonrası agent'a güncel LoRa config gönder\n    const edgeDevice = await this.deviceRepository.findOne({ where: { id: edgeDeviceId } });\n    if (edgeDevice) {\n      await this.pushLoRaConfigToDevice(edgeDevice);\n    }\n\n    return saved;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Remove a LoRa device by ID.\n   * Tenant boundary check yapılır — farklı tenant'ın cihazı silinemez.\n   */\n  async removeLoRaDevice(edgeDeviceId: string, id: string, tenantId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n    const device = await this.loraDeviceRepository.findOne({\n      where: { id, tenantId },\n    });\n    if (!device) {\n      throw new NotFoundException(`LoRa device with ID '${id}' not found`);\n    }\n\n    await this.loraDeviceRepository.delete({ id });\n    this.logger.log(`Removed LoRa device: ${device.name} (DevEUI: ${device.devEui})`);\n\n    // Silme sonrası agent'a güncel LoRa config gönder\n    const edgeDevice = await this.deviceRepository.findOne({ where: { id: edgeDeviceId } });\n    if (edgeDevice) {\n      await this.pushLoRaConfigToDevice(edgeDevice);\n    }\n\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Sadece LoRa config'ini agent'a push et.\n   * addLoRaDevice ve removeLoRaDevice sonrası çağrılır —\n   * agent'ın LoRa cihaz listesini güncel tutmak için.\n   * Device offline ise sessizce atlanır (log ile bildirilir).\n   */\n  private async pushLoRaConfigToDevice(edgeDevice: EdgeDevice): Promise<void> {\n    if (!edgeDevice.isOnline) {\n      this.logger.debug(\n        `Skipping LoRa config push to ${edgeDevice.deviceCode} — device is offline`,\n      );\n      return;\n    }\n\n    let mqtt: MqttClientService;\n    try {\n      mqtt = this.ensureMqttAvailable();\n    } catch {\n      this.logger.warn('MQTT not available — skipping LoRa config push');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const loraConfig = await this.buildLoRaWanConfig(edgeDevice.id);\n\n    // Cihaz listesi boşsa agent'a LoRa modülünü kapatması için boş liste gönder\n    const devices = loraConfig?.devices ?? [];\n\n    try {\n      await mqtt.publish(`tenants/${edgeDevice.tenantId}/devices/${edgeDevice.id}/commands`, {\n        commandId: randomUUID(),\n        command: 'update_lora_devices',\n        params: { devices },\n        timestamp: new Date(),\n      });\n      this.logger.log(`Pushed LoRa config to ${edgeDevice.deviceCode}: ${devices.length} devices`);\n    } catch (error) {\n      this.logger.error(\n        `Failed to push LoRa config to ${edgeDevice.deviceCode}: ${(error as Error).message}`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send a downlink payload to a LoRa end-device via MQTT.\n   *\n   * Edge agent, MQTT command topic'inden gelen downlink komutunu alır\n   * ve SX1302 üzerinden ilgili cihazın bir sonraki RX window'unda\n   * payload'ı iletir. Class A cihazlarda bu bir sonraki uplink'i\n   * bekler; Class C cihazlarda hemen gönderilir.\n   *\n   * @param edgeDeviceId  Edge device (gateway) UUID\n   * @param loraDeviceId  LoRa device UUID\n   * @param payload       Hex string downlink payload\n   * @param fPort         Application port (1-223)\n   * @param tenantId      Tenant isolation\n   * @param confirmed     Confirmed downlink — cihazdan ACK beklenir mi\n   */\n  async sendLoRaDownlink(\n    edgeDeviceId: string,\n    loraDeviceId: string,\n    payload: string,\n    fPort: number,\n    tenantId: string,\n    confirmed = false,\n  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {\n    const loraDevice = await this.loraDeviceRepository.findOne({\n      where: { id: loraDeviceId, tenantId },","numLines":150,"startLine":2240,"totalLines":2745}}}

name: 'DeviceLifecycleState',\n  description: 'Lifecycle state of the edge device',\n});\n\n/**\n * Device model enum\n */\nexport enum DeviceModel {\n  REVOLUTION_PI_CONNECT_4 = 'industrial_pc',\n  CUSTOM = 'custom',\n}\n\nregisterEnumType(DeviceModel, {\n  name: 'DeviceModel',\n  description: 'Hardware model of the edge device',\n});\n\n/**\n * Security level enum (IEC 'mqtt_password_hash', length: 128, nullable: true })\n  mqttPasswordHash!: string | null;\n\n  // Device Fingerprint (collected during activation)\n  @Field(() => GraphQLJSON, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ type: 'jsonb', nullable: true })\n  fingerprint!: {\n    cpuSerial?: string;\n    macAddresses?: string[];\n    machineId?: string;\n    hostname?: string;\n  } | null;\n\n  // Agent Version\n  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ type: 'varchar', name: 'agent_version', length: 30, nullable: true })\n  agentVersion!: string | null;\n\n  // Connection\n  @Field({ firmwareVersion?: string;\n\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'firmware_updated_at', type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })\n  firmwareUpdatedAt?: Date;\n\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'target_firmware_version', length: 30, nullable: true })\n  targetFirmwareVersion?: string;\n\n  // Configuration Sync (SENSOR-HIGH-064)\n  // The I/O config the device most recently CONFIRMED applying, tracked by a\n  // content hash of the pushed agent config + the ack timestamp. Set only when a\n  // real `update_io_config` ack arrives (correlated by commandId), never on\n  // publish — so the operator sees the truthful applied state, not an optimistic\n  // \"pushed\" state.\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'applied_config_hash', type: 'varchar', length: 64, nullable: true })\n  appliedConfigHash?: string;\n\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'last_config_ack_at', type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })\n  lastConfigAckAt?: Date;\n\n  // Health Metrics\n  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'cpu_usage', type: 'int', nullable: true })\n  cpuUsage?: number;\n\n  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'memory_usage', type: 'int', nullable: true })\n  memoryUsage?: number;\n\n  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'storage_usage', type: 'int', nullable: true })\n  storageUsage?: number;\n\n  @Field(() => Float, { nullable: true })\n  // DecimalTransformer: device temperature reported by the edge device for health monitoring.\n  // Temperature thresholds (overheating alerts) use numeric comparison; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R9Quq99ZkVmreJx7RZ3QQp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport {\n2\t  ObjectType,\n3\t  Field,\n4\t  ID,\n5\t  Int,\n6\t  Float,\n7\t  registerEnumType,\n8\t} from '@nestjs/graphql';\n9\timport {\n10\t  Entity,\n11\t  Column,\n12\t  PrimaryGeneratedColumn,\n13\t  CreateDateColumn,\n14\t  UpdateDateColumn,\n15\t  Index,\n16\t  ManyToOne,\n17\t  JoinColumn,\n18\t} from 'typeorm';\n19\t\n20\timport { EdgeDevice } from './edge-device.entity';\n21\timport { EncryptedColumnTransformer } from '../../infrastructure/vault/credential.transformer';\n22\t\n23\t/**\n24\t * LoRaWAN Activation Mode\n25\t *\n26\t * OTAA (Over-The-Air Activation): Cihaz, join request göndererek ağa katılır.\n27\t * Network server, AppKey kullanarak session key'leri türetir. Daha güvenli,\n28\t * endüstriyel ortamda tercih edilir.\n29\t *\n30\t * ABP (Activation By Personalization): Session key'ler cihaza önceden yüklenir.\n31\t * Join prosedürü atlanır. Hızlı kurulum ama güvenlik açısından zayıf —\n32\t * session key'ler hiç değişmez.\n33\t */\n34\texport enum LoRaActivationMode {\n35\t  OTAA = 'OTAA',\n36\t  ABP = 'ABP',\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\tregisterEnumType(LoRaActivationMode, {\n40\t  name: 'LoRaActivationMode',\n41\t  description: 'LoRaWAN activation mode: OTAA (Over-The-Air) or ABP (Activation By Personalization)',\n42\t});\n43\t\n44\t/**\n45\t * LoRaWAN Device Class\n46\t *\n47\t * Class A: Varsayılan. Uplink sonrası iki kısa receive window açılır.\n48\t *          En düşük güç tüketimi. Sensörler için ideal.\n49\t *\n50\t * Class B: Beacon senkronizasyonu ile zamanlanmış receive slot'ları.\n51\t *          Orta güç tüketimi. Periyodik downlink gereken cihazlar için.\n52\t *\n53\t * Class C: Sürekli dinleme modu (TX dışında her zaman RX açık).\n54\t *          En yüksek güç tüketimi. Aktüatörler ve kontrol cihazları için.\n55\t *          Akvakültür vana/pompa kontrolünde Class C tercih edilir.\n56\t */\n57\texport enum LoRaDeviceClass {\n58\t  A = 'A',\n59\t  B = 'B',\n60\t  C = 'C',\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\tregisterEnumType(LoRaDeviceClass, {\n64\t  name: 'LoRaDeviceClass',\n65\t  description: 'LoRaWAN device class: A (lowest power), B (beacon-synced), C (continuous RX)',\n66\t});\n67\t\n68\t/**\n69\t * LoRaDevice entity — LoRaWAN end-device'ı temsil eder.\n70\t *\n71\t * Her LoRa cihazı bir EdgeDevice'a bağlıdır (ManyToOne). EdgeDevice üzerindeki\n72\t * Raspberry Pi + SX1302 HAT, LoRa gateway/concentrator rolünü üstlenir ve\n73\t * bu tablodaki cihazlarla iletişim kurar.\n74\t *\n75\t * LoRaWAN Kavramları:\n76\t * - DevEUI: Globally unique 64-bit cihaz tanımlayıcı (IEEE EUI-64)\n77\t * - AppEUI/JoinEUI: Uygulama tanımlayıcı (OTAA join request'te kullanılır)\n78\t * - AppKey: 128-bit root key (OTAA'da session key türetimi için)\n79\t * - DevAddr: 32-bit ağ adresi (join-accept sonrası veya ABP'de statik)\n80\t * - ADR: Adaptive Data Rate — ağ koşullarına göre SF/BW otomatik ayarı\n81\t * - fPort: Uygulama katmanı port numarası (1-223 arası)\n82\t * - Codec: Payload decode formatı (CayenneLPP, custom binary, vb.)\n83\t */\n84\t@ObjectType()\n85\t@Entity('lora_devices')\n86\t@Index(['tenantId', 'edgeDeviceId'])\n87\t@Index(['devEui'], { unique: true })\n88\texport class LoRaDevice {\n89\t  @Field(() => ID)\n90\t  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')\n91\t  id!: string;\n92\t\n93\t  // Edge Device relation — LoRa gateway olarak görev yapan Pi\n94\t  @Field()\n95\t  @Column({ name: 'edge_device_id' })\n96\t  edgeDeviceId!: string;\n97\t\n98\t  @Field(() => EdgeDevice)\n99\t  @ManyToOne(() => EdgeDevice, { onDelete: 'CASCADE' })\n100\t  @JoinColumn({ name: 'edge_device_id' })\n101\t  edgeDevice!: EdgeDevice;\n102\t\n103\t  // LoRaWAN Identity\n104\t  /** DevEUI: 16 hex karakter (8 byte). Cihazın globally unique tanımlayıcısı. */\n105\t  @Field()\n106\t  @Column({ name: 'dev_eui', type: 'varchar', length: 16 })\n107\t  devEui!: string;\n108\t\n109\t  /** AppEUI/JoinEUI: OTAA aktivasyonu için gerekli. ABP'de opsiyonel. */\n110\t  @Field({ nullable: true })\n111\t  @Column({ name: 'app_eui', type: 'varchar', length: 16, nullable: true })\n112\t  appEui?: string;\n113\t\n114\t  /**\n115\t   * AppKey: 128-bit root key. Daha güvenli,\n * endüstriyel ortamda tercih edilir.\n *\n * ABP (Activation By Personalization): Session key'ler cihaza önceden yüklenir.\n * Join prosedürü atlanır. Hızlı kurulum ama güvenlik açısından zayıf —\n * session key'ler hiç değişmez.\n */\nexport enum LoRaActivationMode {\n  OTAA = 'OTAA',\n  ABP = 'ABP',\n}\n\nregisterEnumType(LoRaActivationMode, {\n  name: 'LoRaActivationMode',\n  description: 'LoRaWAN activation mode: OTAA (Over-The-Air) or ABP (Activation By Personalization)',\n});\n\n/**\n * LoRaWAN Device Class\n *\n * Class A: Varsayılan. Uplink sonrası iki kısa receive window açılır.\n *          En düşük güç tüketimi. Sensörler için ideal.\n *\n * Class B: Beacon senkronizasyonu ile zamanlanmış receive slot'ları.\n *          Orta güç tüketimi. Periyodik downlink gereken cihazlar için.\n *\n * Class C: Sürekli dinleme modu (TX dışında her zaman RX açık).\n *          En yüksek güç tüketimi. Aktüatörler ve kontrol cihazları için.\n *          Akvakültür vana/pompa kontrolünde Class C tercih edilir.\n */\nexport enum LoRaDeviceClass {\n  A = 'A',\n  B = 'B',\n  C = 'C',\n}\n\nregisterEnumType(LoRaDeviceClass, {\n  name: 'LoRaDeviceClass',\n  description: 'LoRaWAN device class: A (lowest power), B (beacon-synced), C (continuous RX)',\n});\n\n/**\n * LoRaDevice entity — LoRaWAN end-device'ı temsil eder.\n *\n * Her LoRa cihazı bir EdgeDevice'a bağlıdır (ManyToOne). EdgeDevice üzerindeki\n * Raspberry Pi + SX1302 HAT, LoRa gateway/concentrator rolünü üstlenir ve\n * bu tablodaki cihazlarla iletişim kurar.\n *\n * LoRaWAN Kavramları:\n * - DevEUI: Globally unique 64-bit cihaz tanımlayıcı (IEEE EUI-64)\n * - AppEUI/JoinEUI: Uygulama tanımlayıcı (OTAA join request'te kullanılır)\n * - AppKey: 128-bit root key (OTAA'da session key türetimi için)\n * - DevAddr: 32-bit ağ adresi (join-accept sonrası veya ABP'de statik)\n * - ADR: Adaptive Data Rate — ağ koşullarına göre SF/BW otomatik ayarı\n * - fPort: Uygulama katmanı port numarası (1-223 arası)\n * - Codec: Payload decode formatı (CayenneLPP, custom binary, vb.)\n */\n@ObjectType()\n@Entity('lora_devices')\n@Index(['tenantId', 'edgeDeviceId'])\n@Index(['devEui'], { unique: true })\nexport class LoRaDevice {\n  @Field(() => ID)\n  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')\n  id!: string;\n\n  // Edge Device relation — LoRa gateway olarak görev yapan Pi\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ name: 'edge_device_id' })\n  edgeDeviceId!: string;\n\n  @Field(() => EdgeDevice)\n  @ManyToOne(() => EdgeDevice, { onDelete: 'CASCADE' })\n  @JoinColumn({ name: 'edge_device_id' })\n  edgeDevice!: EdgeDevice;\n\n  // LoRaWAN Identity\n  /** DevEUI: 16 hex karakter (8 byte). Cihazın globally unique tanımlayıcısı. */\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ name: 'dev_eui', type: 'varchar', length: 16 })\n  devEui!: string;\n\n  /** AppEUI/JoinEUI: OTAA aktivasyonu için gerekli. ABP'de opsiyonel. */\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'app_eui', type: 'varchar', length: 16, nullable: true })\n  appEui?: string;\n\n  /**\n   * AppKey: 128-bit root key. OTAA join prosedüründe session key türetimi için kullanılır.\n   * AES-256-GCM ile uygulama katmanında şifrelenerek saklanır (EncryptedColumnTransformer).\n   * Format: enc:<iv_hex>:<authTag_hex>:<ciphertext_hex>\n   */\n  @Column({ name: 'app_key', type: 'varchar', length: 255, transformer: EncryptedColumnTransformer })\n  appKey!: string;\n\n  /** Maskelenmiş AppKey — GraphQL'den güvenli erişim için ilk 4 ve son 4 karakter gösterilir */\n  @Field({ description: 'Masked application key (first 4 + last 4 chars)' })\n  get appKeyMasked(): string {\n    if (!this.appKey) return '';\n    return `${this.appKey.slice(0, 4)}${'*'.repeat(24)}${this.appKey.slice(-4)}`;\n  }\n\n  /** DevAddr: Join-accept sonrası atanan veya ABP'de statik olarak yapılandırılan ağ adresi. */\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'dev_addr', type: 'varchar', length: 8, nullable: true })\n  devAddr?: string;\n\n  // Activation & Class\n  @Field(() => LoRaActivationMode)\n  @Column({ name: 'activation_mode', type: 'enum', enum: LoRaActivationMode, default: LoRaActivationMode.OTAA })\n  activationMode!: LoRaActivationMode;\n\n  @Field(() => LoRaDeviceClass)\n  @Column({ name: 'device_class', type: 'enum', enum: LoRaDeviceClass, default: LoRaDeviceClass.A })\n  deviceClass!: LoRaDeviceClass;\n\n  // User-friendly fields\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 50 })\n  name!: string;\n\n  /**\n   * tagPrefix: I/O tag isimlendirme ön eki. Edge agent, bu prefix ile\n   * LoRa cihazından gelen decoded değerleri tag olarak publish eder.\n   * Ör: tagPrefix=\"LORA_PH\" → \"LORA_PH_temperature\", \"LORA_PH_humidity\"\n   */\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ name: 'tag_prefix', type: 'varchar', length: 30 })\n  tagPrefix!: string;\n\n  /**\n   * Codec: Payload decode formatı.\n   * - cayenne_lpp: Standart CayenneLPP (IPSO Smart Objects tabanlı)\n   * - raw: Ham byte dizisi, decode edge agent'ta custom handler ile yapılır\n   * - json: JSON payload (nadir, genellikle Class C cihazlarda)\n   */\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 20, default: 'cayenne_lpp' })\n  codec!: string;\n\n  // Radio parameters\n  /** ADR: Adaptive Data Rate. LoRa ağ sunucusu SF ve BW'yi otomatik optimize eder. */\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ name: 'adr_enabled', default: true })\n  adrEnabled!: boolean;\n\n  /** fPort: LoRaWAN uygulama katmanı port numarası. Farklı payload tipleri için ayrılabilir. */\n  @Field(() => Int)\n  @Column({ name: 'f_port', type: 'smallint', default: 1 })\n  fPort!: number;\n\n  // Runtime status — edge agent tarafından güncellenir\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'last_seen_at', type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })\n  lastSeenAt?: Date;\n\n  /** RSSI (Received Signal Strength Indicator): Alınan sinyal gücü (dBm). Tipik: -120 ile -30 arası. */\n  @Field(() => Float, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'last_rssi', type: 'real', nullable: true })\n  lastRssi?: number;\n\n  /** SNR (Signal-to-Noise Ratio): Sinyal/gürültü oranı (dB). Pozitif değerler iyi bağlantı gösterir. */\n  @Field(() => Float, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'last_snr', type: 'real', nullable: true })\n  lastSnr?: number;\n\n  /** Uplink frame counter. LoRaWAN güvenliği için her uplink'te artırılır. Replay attack koruması sağlar. */\n  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'frame_count_up', type: 'int', nullable: true })\n  frameCountUp?: number;\n\n  /** Join durumu: OTAA ile başarılı join-accept alındıysa true. */\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ name: 'is_joined', default: false })\n  isJoined!: boolean;\n\n  @Field({ nullable: true })\n  @Column({ name: 'joined_at', type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })\n  joinedAt?: Date;\n\n  // Tenant isolation\n  @Field()\n  @Column({ name: 'tenant_id', type: 'uuid' })\n  tenantId!: string;\n\n  // Timestamps\n  @Field()\n  @CreateDateColumn({ name: 'created_at' })\n  createdAt!: Date;\n\n  @Field()\n  @UpdateDateColumn({ name: 'updated_at' })\n  updatedAt!: Date;\n}\n","numLines":221,"startLine":1,"totalLines":221}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01L8XJDTTEWyKtVBfZVs7g36","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n2\t\n3\timport {\n4\t  DEV_FALLBACK_KEY,\n5\t  decryptSecretValue,\n6\t  encryptSecretValue,\n7\t  resolveEncryptionKey,\n8\t} from '../../infrastructure/vault/credential-crypto';\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * SENSOR-MEDIUM-044 — encrypt existing plaintext LoRaWAN AppKeys at rest.\n12\t *\n13\t * `lora_devices.app_key` is the 128-bit OTAA root key — the LoRaWAN root of\n14\t * trust; a DB read or backup dump of a plaintext key lets an attacker\n15\t * impersonate or decrypt end devices. The column already carries the AES-256-GCM\n16\t * `EncryptedColumnTransformer` (new writes are encrypted, added in #941), but\n17\t * rows written before it remain plaintext. This is the blue-green backfill for\n18\t * those rows.\n19\t *\n20\t * Data-only migration (no DDL): UPDATE over `lora_devices` in the canonical\n21\t * `sensor` source schema AND every provisioned `tenant_*` schema (the sensor\n22\t * runner invokes migrations once, so the fan-out is explicit). Idempotent +\n23\t * re-runnable — only rows whose `app_key` is not already in `enc:` form are\n24\t * rewritten. Uses the same `CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY` and byte-identical `enc:`\n25\t * format as the runtime `CredentialVaultService`, resolved lazily so a table\n26\t * with no plaintext key (fresh CI DB) needs no key at all; production fails\n27\t * closed when a key must be used but none is configured.\n28\t */\n29\texport class EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest1812000000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n30\t  name = 'EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest1812000000000';\n31\t\n32\t  private cachedKey: Buffer | null = null;\n33\t\n34\t  private resolveKey(): Buffer {\n35\t    if (this.cachedKey) {\n36\t      return this.cachedKey;\n37\t    }\n38\t    const resolved = resolveEncryptionKey(process.env['CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY']);\n39\t    if (resolved) {\n40\t      this.cachedKey = resolved;\n41\t      return resolved;\n42\t    }\n43\t    if (process.env['NODE_ENV'] === 'production') {\n44\t      throw new Error(\n45\t        '[EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest] CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY is required in production ' +\n46\t          'to encrypt existing LoRaWAN AppKeys at rest.',\n47\t      );\n48\t    }\n49\t    this.cachedKey = Buffer.from(DEV_FALLBACK_KEY, 'utf8');\n50\t    return this.cachedKey;\n51\t  }\n52\t\n53\t  private async loraSchemas(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<string[]> {\n54\t    const rows: Array<{ nspname: string }> = await queryRunner.query(\n55\t      `SELECT nspname FROM pg_namespace WHERE nspname = 'sensor' OR nspname LIKE 'tenant\\\\_%'`,\n56\t    );\n57\t    const schemas: string[] = [];\n58\t    for (const { nspname } of rows) {\n59\t      const exists: unknown[] = await queryRunner.query(\n60\t        `SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = 'lora_devices'`,\n61\t        [nspname],\n62\t      );\n63\t      if (exists.length > 0) {\n64\t        schemas.push(nspname);\n65\t      }\n66\t    }\n67\t    return schemas;\n68\t  }\n69\t\n70\t  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n71\t    for (const schema of await this.loraSchemas(queryRunner)) {\n72\t      // Only plaintext rows — already-`enc:` keys are skipped (idempotent).\n73\t      const rows: Array<{ id: string; app_key: string }> = await queryRunner.query(\n74\t        `SELECT id, app_key FROM \"${schema}\".\"lora_devices\" WHERE app_key IS NOT NULL AND app_key NOT LIKE 'enc:%'`,\n75\t      );\n76\t      for (const row of rows) {\n77\t        await queryRunner.query(\n78\t          `UPDATE \"${schema}\".\"lora_devices\" SET app_key = $1 WHERE id = $2`,\n79\t          [encryptSecretValue(row.app_key, this.resolveKey()), row.id],\n80\t        );"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d54a3eac-18fd-4a7e-9136-0fe6978db2bc","uuid":"62386f15-3b38-4259-b0f7-c49eb48b3fc7","timestamp":"2026-10-08T02:25:16.965Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-244ce119d6a7/apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations/1812000000000-EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest.ts","content":"import { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n\nimport {\n  DEV_FALLBACK_KEY,\n  decryptSecretValue,\n  encryptSecretValue,\n  resolveEncryptionKey,\n} from '../../infrastructure/vault/credential-crypto';\n\n/**\n * SENSOR-MEDIUM-044 — encrypt existing plaintext LoRaWAN AppKeys at rest.\n *\n * `lora_devices.app_key` is the 128-bit OTAA root key — the LoRaWAN root of\n * trust; a DB read or backup dump of a plaintext key lets an attacker\n * impersonate or decrypt end devices. The column already carries the AES-256-GCM\n * `EncryptedColumnTransformer` (new writes are encrypted, added in #941), but\n * rows written before it remain plaintext. This is the blue-green backfill for\n * those rows.\n *\n * Data-only migration (no DDL): UPDATE over `lora_devices` in the canonical\n * `sensor` source schema AND every provisioned `tenant_*` schema (the sensor\n * runner invokes migrations once, so the fan-out is explicit). Idempotent +\n * re-runnable — only rows whose `app_key` is not already in `enc:` form are\n * rewritten. Uses the same `CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY` and byte-identical `enc:`\n * format as the runtime `CredentialVaultService`, resolved lazily so a table\n * with no plaintext key (fresh CI DB) needs no key at all; production fails\n * closed when a key must be used but none is configured.\n */\nexport class EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest1812000000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n  name = 'EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest1812000000000';\n\n  private cachedKey: Buffer | null = null;\n\n  private resolveKey(): Buffer {\n    if (this.cachedKey) {\n      return this.cachedKey;\n    }\n    const resolved = resolveEncryptionKey(process.env['CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY']);\n    if (resolved) {\n      this.cachedKey = resolved;\n      return resolved;\n    }\n    if (process.env['NODE_ENV'] === 'production') {\n      throw new Error(\n        '[EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest] CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY is required in production ' +\n          'to encrypt existing LoRaWAN AppKeys at rest.',\n      );\n    }\n    this.cachedKey = Buffer.from(DEV_FALLBACK_KEY, 'utf8');\n    return this.cachedKey;\n  }\n\n  private async loraSchemas(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<string[]> {\n    const rows: Array<{ nspname: string }> = await queryRunner.query(\n      `SELECT nspname FROM pg_namespace WHERE nspname = 'sensor' OR nspname LIKE 'tenant\\\\_%'`,\n    );\n    const schemas: string[] = [];\n    for (const { nspname } of rows) {\n      const exists: unknown[] = await queryRunner.query(\n        `SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_name = 'lora_devices'`,\n        [nspname],\n      );\n      if (exists.length > 0) {\n        schemas.push(nspname);\n      }\n    }\n    return schemas;\n  }\n\n  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n    for (const schema of await this.loraSchemas(queryRunner)) {\n      // Only plaintext rows — already-`enc:` keys are skipped (idempotent).\n      const rows: Array<{ id: string; app_key: string }> = await queryRunner.query(\n        `SELECT id, app_key FROM \"${schema}\".\"lora_devices\" WHERE app_key IS NOT NULL AND app_key NOT LIKE 'enc:%'`,\n      );\n      for (const row of rows) {\n        await queryRunner.query(\n          `UPDATE \"${schema}\".\"lora_devices\" SET app_key = $1 WHERE id = $2`,\n          [encryptSecretValue(row.app_key, this.resolveKey()), row.id],\n        );","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":101}}}

  * Mark devices as offline if no heartbeat received.\n   *\n   * Iterates ALL tenant schemas so that devices in every tenant are checked,\n   * not just the default search_path (which would only hit the `sensor` schema).\n   */\n  /**\n   * The scheduled entry point. Separate from the method below because a\n   * scheduled tick takes no arguments and returns nothing — the timeout is a\n   * parameter of the operation, not of the schedule, and the affected count is\n   * a result for a caller that asked.\n   */\n  @ScheduledJob({ name: 'edge-device.mark-stale-offline', every: 60_000 })\n  async markStaleDevicesOfflineTick(): Promise<void> {\n    await this.markStaleDevicesOffline();\n  }\n\n  async markStaleDevicesOffline(timeoutMinutes = 5): Promise<number> {\n    const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - timeoutMinutes * 60 * 1000);\n    let totalAffected = 0;\n\n    let schemas: string[];\n    try {\n      schemas = await listTenantSchemas(this.dataSource);\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger.error(\n        `Failed to fetch tenant schemas for stale-device check: ${(err as Error).message}`,\n      );\n      return 0;\n    }\n\n    for (const schemaName of schemas) {\n      const qr = this.dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n      try {\n        await qr.connect();\n        await qr.startTransaction();\n        await pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath(qr, 'sensor', schemaName);\n\n        const result: unknown = await qr.query(\n          `UPDATE edge_devices\n           SET is_online = false,\n               lifecycle_state = $1\n           WHERE is_online = true\n             AND last_seen_at < $2\n             AND lifecycle_state NOT IN ($3, $4)`,\n          [\n            DeviceLifecycleState.OFFLINE,\n            cutoff.toISOString(),\n            DeviceLifecycleState.DECOMMISSIONED,\n            DeviceLifecycleState.MAINTENANCE,\n          ],\n        );\n\n        const affected = EdgeDeviceService.queryAffectedRows(result);\n        if (affected > 0) {\n          this.logger.log(`Marked ${affected} devices as offline in ${schemaName}`);\n          totalAffected += affected;\n        }\n        await qr.commitTransaction();\n      } catch (err) {\n        if (qr.isTransactionActive) {\n          await qr.rollbackTransaction().catch(() => undefined);\n        }\n        this.logger.error(\n          `Failed stale-device check for ${schemaName}: ${(err as Error).message}`,\n        );\n      } finally {\n        await qr.release();\n      }\n    }\n","numLines":70,"startLine":800,"totalLines":2745}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WBwJm6VkuuXoj7doMBbyN9","type":"tool_result","content":"170\t    }),\n171\t    // CIRCUIT-LOW-002 cure: register the canonical\n172\t    // CircuitBreakerService at the @Global module level so the\n173\t    // sensor-protocol HttpRestAdapter and the channel-detection\n174\t    // service can constructor-inject it without per-feature-module\n175\t    // re-imports. Same pattern admin-api uses for CIRCUIT-LOW-001.\n176\t    CircuitBreakerModule,\n177\t\n178\t    // Database connection — sensor-service owns the 'sensor' schema (over\n179\t    // TimescaleDB). Uses the platform TypeORM factory.\n180\t    // INTENTIONAL: no `schema:` — TenantConnectionBootstrap manages\n181\t    // search_path per request.\n182\t    //\n183\t    // MEDIUM-006: pool sized 50 / min 10 / idle 5min for continuous MQTT\n184\t    // ingestion — connection cold-starts during burst ingest cause\n185\t    // >100ms tail-latency spikes. Idle 5min prevents pool churn between\n186\t    // bursts. Operators may further tune via DATABASE_POOL_* env vars.\n187\t    //\n188\t    // Legacy TIMESCALE_* env-var fallbacks were removed — they were not\n189\t    // set anywhere in the platform (compose / helm / .env). Operator\n190\t    // config flows through DATABASE_* uniformly.\n191\t    TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({\n192\t      imports: [ConfigModule],\n193\t      inject: [ConfigService],\n194\t      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) =>\n195\t        createServiceTypeOrmConfig(configService, {\n196\t          serviceName: 'sensor',\n197\t          schema: 'sensor',\n198\t          defaultPoolSize: 50,\n199\t          defaultPoolMin: 10,\n200\t          defaultPoolIdleTimeoutMs: 300_000,\n201\t          subscribers: [AuditSubscriber],\n202\t          entities: [\n203\t            // SensorOutbox must be in TypeORM metadata: this service passes an\n204\t            // explicit entities list, so autoLoadEntities is off and OutboxModule.\n205\t            // forFeature() alone does not register the entity. Without it the new\n206\t            // OutboxNotifyListener.onModuleInit getMetadata(SensorOutbox) throws\n207\t            // (\"No metadata for SensorOutbox\"), crash-looping sensor-service boot.\n208\t            SensorOutbox,\n209\t            // ADR-0006: shared.access_logs — written by the factory-mounted"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d54a3eac-18fd-4a7e-9136-0fe6978db2bc","uuid":"322cfb25-d128-4bab-89f7-5d31d7ef7d40","timestamp":"2026-10-08T02:25:56.623Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-244ce119d6a7/apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts","content":"    }),\n    // CIRCUIT-LOW-002 cure: register the canonical\n    // CircuitBreakerService at the @Global module level so the\n    // sensor-protocol HttpRestAdapter and the channel-detection\n    // service can constructor-inject it without per-feature-module\n    // re-imports. Same pattern admin-api uses for CIRCUIT-LOW-001.\n    CircuitBreakerModule,\n\n    // Database connection — sensor-service owns the 'sensor' schema (over\n    // TimescaleDB). Uses the platform TypeORM factory.\n    // INTENTIONAL: no `schema:` — TenantConnectionBootstrap manages\n    // search_path per request.\n    //\n    // MEDIUM-006: pool sized 50 / min 10 / idle 5min for continuous MQTT\n    // ingestion — connection cold-starts during burst ingest cause\n    // >100ms tail-latency spikes. Idle 5min prevents pool churn between\n    // bursts. Operators may further tune via DATABASE_POOL_* env vars.\n    //\n    // Legacy TIMESCALE_* env-var fallbacks were removed — they were not\n    // set anywhere in the platform (compose / helm / .env). Operator\n    // config flows through DATABASE_* uniformly.\n    TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({\n      imports: [ConfigModule],\n      inject: [ConfigService],\n      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) =>\n        createServiceTypeOrmConfig(configService, {\n          serviceName: 'sensor',\n          schema: 'sensor',\n          defaultPoolSize: 50,\n          defaultPoolMin: 10,\n          defaultPoolIdleTimeoutMs: 300_000,\n          subscribers: [AuditSubscriber],\n          entities: [\n            // SensorOutbox must be in TypeORM metadata: this service passes an\n            // explicit entities list, so autoLoadEntities is off and OutboxModule.\n            // forFeature() alone does not register the entity. Without it the new\n            // OutboxNotifyListener.onModuleInit getMetadata(SensorOutbox) throws\n            // (\"No metadata for SensorOutbox\"), crash-looping sensor-service boot.\n            SensorOutbox,\n            // ADR-0006: shared.access_logs — written by the factory-mounted","numLines":40,"startLine":170,"totalLines":593}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JCD4ANhACMGJ4xpGEdUKKo","type":"tool_result","content":"525\t    // SENSOR-HIGH-022: fine-grained tenant permission guard. It is opt-in — a\n526\t    // handler with no @RequireTenantPermission passes through untouched — so a\n527\t    // global registration is safe and makes every @RequireTenantPermission\n528\t    // (e.g. 'edge:manage-io-config') self-enforcing instead of dead metadata.\n529\t    {\n530\t      provide: APP_GUARD,\n531\t      useFactory: (reflector: Reflector): TenantPermissionGuard =>\n532\t        new TenantPermissionGuard(reflector),\n533\t      inject: [Reflector],\n534\t    },\n535\t    // Bootstrap source schema tables on startup (creates template tables if missing)\n536\t    SourceSchemaBootstrapService,\n537\t    // Pool-level tenant schema routing (patches pg Pool.connect for search_path injection)\n538\t    TenantConnectionBootstrap,\n539\t    // Auto-sync tenant schemas with source schema (creates missing tables/columns)\n540\t    TenantSchemaSyncService,\n541\t  ],\n542\t})\n543\texport class AppModule implements NestModule {\n544\t  configure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer): void {\n545\t    // SEC-CRITICAL-002 sweep — strips forged internal headers when the request\n546\t    // lacks a valid x-service-identity HMAC. Safe on every route (only deletes\n547\t    // spoofable headers), so it + metrics stay on '*'.\n548\t    consumer.apply(StripInternalHeadersMiddleware, MetricsMiddleware).forRoutes('*');\n549\t\n550\t    // SEC-HIGH-156 — resolve req.user/req.tenantId from the gateway-signed\n551\t    // verified-user assertion (runs after Strip sets req.verifiedIdentity,\n552\t    // before UserContext). EXCLUDED from the MQTT auth routes\n553\t    // (edge-device/mqtt-auth.controller.ts): Mosquitto's go-auth plugin calls\n554\t    // /mqtt/{auth,superuser,acl} with NO gateway service identity, so requiring\n555\t    // one there would 500 the broker. Both the prefix-stripped (/mqtt/*) and any\n556\t    // prefixed form are excluded to fail safe.\n557\t    consumer\n558\t      .apply(VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware)\n559\t      // Non-gateway public surfaces that carry NO gateway service identity and\n560\t      // would otherwise 400 in production (requiresServiceIdentity):\n561\t      //  - /mqtt/*           Mosquitto go-auth plugin (edge-device/mqtt-auth.controller)\n562\t      //  - /install/*, /api/devices/*  edge-device provisioning + activation,\n563\t      //    called DIRECTLY by edge agents (nginx proxies these to sensor,\n564\t      //    bypassing the gateway — edge-device/provisioning.controller, @Public()).\n565\t      // Both prefix-stripped and api/v1-prefixed forms are excluded to fail safe.\n566\t      .exclude(\n567\t        'mqtt',\n568\t        'mqtt/{*path}',\n569\t        'api/v1/mqtt',\n570\t        'api/v1/mqtt/{*path}',\n571\t        'install',\n572\t        'install/{*path}',\n573\t        'api/v1/install',\n574\t        'api/v1/install/{*path}',\n575\t        'api/devices',\n576\t        'api/devices/{*path}',\n577\t        'api/v1/api/devices',\n578\t        'api/v1/api/devices/{*path}',\n579\t      )\n580\t      .forRoutes('*');\n581\t\n582\t    consumer\n583\t      .apply(\n584\t        CorrelationIdMiddleware,\n585\t        RequestContextMiddleware, // Populate AsyncLocalStorage for structured logging\n586\t        UserContextMiddleware,\n587\t        TenantContextMiddleware,\n588\t        TenantSchemaMiddleware, // Sets PostgreSQL search_path to tenant schema\n589\t      )\n590\t      .forRoutes('*');\n591\t  }\n592\t}\n593\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d54a3eac-18fd-4a7e-9136-0fe6978db2bc","uuid":"602581a2-7e5e-434e-8cce-720ee95f1c6c","timestamp":"2026-10-08T02:25:57.816Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-244ce119d6a7/apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts","content":"    // SENSOR-HIGH-022: fine-grained tenant permission guard. It is opt-in — a\n    // handler with no @RequireTenantPermission passes through untouched — so a\n    // global registration is safe and makes every @RequireTenantPermission\n    // (e.g. 'edge:manage-io-config') self-enforcing instead of dead metadata.\n    {\n      provide: APP_GUARD,\n      useFactory: (reflector: Reflector): TenantPermissionGuard =>\n        new TenantPermissionGuard(reflector),\n      inject: [Reflector],\n    },\n    // Bootstrap source schema tables on startup (creates template tables if missing)\n    SourceSchemaBootstrapService,\n    // Pool-level tenant schema routing (patches pg Pool.connect for search_path injection)\n    TenantConnectionBootstrap,\n    // Auto-sync tenant schemas with source schema (creates missing tables/columns)\n    TenantSchemaSyncService,\n  ],\n})\nexport class AppModule implements NestModule {\n  configure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer): void {\n    // SEC-CRITICAL-002 sweep — strips forged internal headers when the request\n    // lacks a valid x-service-identity HMAC. Safe on every route (only deletes\n    // spoofable headers), so it + metrics stay on '*'.\n    consumer.apply(StripInternalHeadersMiddleware, MetricsMiddleware).forRoutes('*');\n\n    // SEC-HIGH-156 — resolve req.user/req.tenantId from the gateway-signed\n    // verified-user assertion (runs after Strip sets req.verifiedIdentity,\n    // before UserContext). EXCLUDED from the MQTT auth routes\n    // (edge-device/mqtt-auth.controller.ts): Mosquitto's go-auth plugin calls\n    // /mqtt/{auth,superuser,acl} with NO gateway service identity, so requiring\n    // one there would 500 the broker. Both the prefix-stripped (/mqtt/*) and any\n    // prefixed form are excluded to fail safe.\n    consumer\n      .apply(VerifiedUserAssertionMiddleware)\n      // Non-gateway public surfaces that carry NO gateway service identity and\n      // would otherwise 400 in production (requiresServiceIdentity):\n      //  - /mqtt/*           Mosquitto go-auth plugin (edge-device/mqtt-auth.controller)\n      //  - /install/*, /api/devices/*  edge-device provisioning + activation,\n      //    called DIRECTLY by edge agents (nginx proxies these to sensor,\n      //    bypassing the gateway — edge-device/provisioning.controller, @Public()).\n      // Both prefix-stripped and api/v1-prefixed forms are excluded to fail safe.\n      .exclude(\n        'mqtt',\n        'mqtt/{*path}',\n        'api/v1/mqtt',\n        'api/v1/mqtt/{*path}',\n        'install',\n        'install/{*path}',\n        'api/v1/install',\n        'api/v1/install/{*path}',\n        'api/devices',\n        'api/devices/{*path}',\n        'api/v1/api/devices',\n        'api/v1/api/devices/{*path}',\n      )\n      .forRoutes('*');\n\n    consumer\n      .apply(\n        CorrelationIdMiddleware,\n        RequestContextMiddleware, // Populate AsyncLocalStorage for structured logging\n        UserContextMiddleware,\n        TenantContextMiddleware,\n        TenantSchemaMiddleware, // Sets PostgreSQL search_path to tenant schema\n      )\n      .forRoutes('*');\n  }\n}\n","numLines":69,"startLine":525,"totalLines":593}}}

In-file proof: the service imports `listTenantSchemas` and `pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath` (line 6), enumerates the provisioned tenant schemas (line 822), pins a transaction-local search_path to one tenant schema at a time (line 835), and then issues an UNQUALIFIED `UPDATE edge_devices` (line 838) — an unqualified table name that resolves per tenant only because each tenant schema carries its own copy of these device tables; the schema name is derived from the tenant id (line 2592). Corroboration consulted outside the request's cited ref set, therefore named in prose rather than cited: `apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/entities/lora-device.entity.ts:85` declares `@Entity('lora_devices')` with NO `schema:` (the per-tenant-table convention), `apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations/1812000000000-EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest.ts:53-78` fans a data migration out over the `sensor` source schema PLUS every provisioned `tenant_*` schema that holds `lora_devices`, and `apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:180-181,537-538,588` wires request-scoped routing (`TenantConnectionBootstrap` patches pg pool checkout; `TenantSchemaMiddleware` sets the search_path on every route). The `tenant_id` column on the entity is a second discriminator layered on top of schema isolation, not the isolation mechanism itself, so the premise's shared-table requirement does not hold.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:6\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:822\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:835\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:838\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2592\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"premise:2\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"The caller DOES restrict the call to the current tenant, five lines above the cited line. `removeLoRaDevice(edgeDeviceId, id, tenantId)` (line 2303) loads the target row with the tenant inside the predicate — `findOne({ where: { id, tenantId } })` (lines 2304-2305) — and throws `NotFoundException` (lines 2307-2308) when no row matches that id+tenant pair. Only after that gate does line 2311 run `delete({ id })`, keyed by the server-generated UUID primary key of the row just proven to belong to the calling tenant. A caller holding another tenant's row id never reaches line 2311: the guard fails closed first. The premise asserts that neither the predicate, a helper, RLS, nor the caller restricts the call; the caller restriction is present and executes unconditionally.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2303\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2304\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2305\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2307\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2308\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"defect\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"No product code or configuration at this location has to change for the defect claim to be false: it is already false. The claim is that a repository call can read or change another tenant's rows; at line 2311 the reachable row set is exactly one row, already matched on `{ id, tenantId }` at line 2305 and inside a connection whose search_path is pinned to one tenant schema (the routing the same file exercises at line 835). Adding `tenantId` to the delete predicate would restate a check that already executes, not remove a reachable cross-tenant write, so there is no defect to resolve here.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2305\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2308\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:835\"\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"verdict\",\n      \"verdict\": \"contradicted\",\n      \"note\": \"false_positive. Both premise obligations and the defect obligation are contradicted by the product source at the finding's location: the table is per-tenant and search_path-routed (lines 6, 822, 835, 838, 2592), and the enclosing method restricts the call to the calling tenant before it fires (lines 2303-2311). Mapped to this contract's closed verdict set, false_positive is reported as `contradicted`.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2305\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:835\"\n      ]\n    }\n  ],\n  \"evidence_refs\": [\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:6\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:822\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:835\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:838\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2303\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2304\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2305\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2307\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2308\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\",\n    \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2592\"\n  ],\n  \"details\": {\n    \"excerpt_check\": {\n      \"pinned_excerpt_lines\": \"1-291\",\n      \"content_hash\": \"sha256:d206414685e4bb1b2d07fee32925b501544dbe9e243aae347e666fb26b8292da\",\n      \"sufficient\": false,\n      \"why\": \"The pinned excerpt is truncated at line 291 and does not contain the cited line 2311, so the file was read at the snapshot. The excerpt head matched what was read (tenant-schema helpers imported at lines 4-6).\",\n      \"identity_fields_not_supplied_by_route\": [\"run_id\", \"prompt_hash\"]\n    },\n    \"verdict\": {\n      \"tool_id\": \"tenant-scoping-adapter\",\n      \"run_id\": null,\n      \"finding_id\": \"tenant-repository-unscoped-read:apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\",\n      \"verdict\": \"false_positive\",\n      \"judge_id\": \"aria-evidence-judge\",\n      \"model\": \"claude-opus-5\",\n      \"prompt_hash\": null,\n      \"confidence\": 0.93,\n      \"judgment_group_id\": \"tenant-repository-unscoped-read:apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\",\n      \"rationale\": \"WHAT WAS JUDGED. The question is the product at the cited line, never whether the rule's predicate matched. The rule matched a true syntactic fact — line 2311 is `await this.loraDeviceRepository.delete({ id })`, with no tenant term in its own predicate — and that fact is given. The obligations ask something different: is the row set that call can touch actually unconstrained by tenant, and must a person change product code or configuration to fix it. The pinned excerpt covers lines 1-291 and does not reach line 2311, so the file was read at the snapshot; its head matched the excerpt (the tenant-schema helpers imported at lines 4-6).\\n\\nPREMISE 2 FAILS — THE CALLER IS THE SCOPE. `removeLoRaDevice(edgeDeviceId, id, tenantId)` (line 2303) receives the tenant as a parameter, loads the row with the tenant IN the predicate — `findOne({ where: { id, tenantId } })` (2304-2305) — and throws `NotFoundException` (2307-2308) when that pair matches nothing. Line 2311 then deletes by the UUID primary key of the row that guard just proved belongs to the calling tenant. The sequence is check-then-act on the same server-generated primary key, so the delete's reachable row set is exactly the one row already authorised. Premise 2 denies that the predicate, a helper, RLS or the caller restricts the call; the caller restriction is present, unconditional, and five lines above the cited call.\\n\\nPREMISE 1 FAILS — THE TABLE IS PER-TENANT, ROUTED BY search_path. `lora_devices` is not a table whose rows sit side by side across tenants. The same file proves the routing model it lives under: it imports `listTenantSchemas` and `pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath` (line 6), enumerates provisioned tenant schemas (822), pins a transaction-local search_path to one schema at a time (835), and then runs an UNQUALIFIED `UPDATE edge_devices` (838) — unqualified precisely because each tenant schema holds its own copy of these device tables — with the schema name derived from the tenant id (2592). Consulted as corroboration but outside the request's cited ref set, so named here rather than cited: `apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/entities/lora-device.entity.ts:85` declares `@Entity('lora_devices')` with no `schema:` (this repository's per-tenant-table convention), `apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations/1812000000000-EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest.ts:53-78` fans a data migration over the `sensor` source schema PLUS every provisioned `tenant_*` schema that contains `lora_devices`, and `apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:180-181,537-538,588` wires the request-scoped pin (`TenantConnectionBootstrap` patches pg pool checkout; `TenantSchemaMiddleware` sets the search_path on every route). The entity's `tenant_id` column is a second discriminator layered over schema isolation, not the isolation mechanism.\\n\\nDEFECT FAILS — NOTHING MUST CHANGE HERE. Two independent mechanisms already make a cross-tenant delete unreachable at this line: physical schema routing, and the tenant-scoped guard on the same primary key. Adding `tenantId` to the delete predicate would restate an enforced check, not close a reachable write. The defect claim 'A repository call can read or change another tenant's rows' does not hold at this location, so the finding is a false positive.\\n\\nWHY THE DISTINCTION MATTERS DOWNSTREAM. This verdict is consumed by `feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus` and rolls into the adapter's precision metric and goldset. Marking a guarded delete-by-primary-key as a true positive would mint a change request that alters no behaviour and would teach the corpus that the guard-then-act shape is a defect — a shape used across sibling services in this repository, so the noise multiplies per service rather than staying local, and the same rule's earlier samples on this very file already consumed operator adjudication. The detector repair that raises precision is to widen the rule's window from the single call to the enclosing method: treat a tenant-scoped read of the same primary key, or an entity declared without `schema:` under per-request search_path pinning, as satisfying the tenant constraint. That repair belongs in the adapter's own lane, not in product code.\\n\\nCONFIDENCE. Held at 0.93: the verdict rests on two independent, directly read grounds, and the guard sits in the same method as the cited call. The residual uncertainty is the narrow reading in which a reviewer wants `tenantId` on the delete predicate as a second barrier against a future refactor that drops the guard — a hardening preference, not the stated defect, and premise 2 explicitly admits caller-side restriction.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:6\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:822\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:835\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:838\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2303\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2304\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2305\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2307\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2308\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2592\"\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\n```"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3941,"cache_read_input_tokens":71506,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3941},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d54a3eac-18fd-4a7e-9136-0fe6978db2bc","uuid":"49345106-4caa-48ec-a12d-0bb26ad79abf","timestamp":"2026-10-08T02:28:47.681Z","request_id":"req_011Cfoyg7ybuAYwTauYA3ndn"}

The question is the product at the cited line, never whether the rule's predicate matched. The rule matched a true syntactic fact — line 2311 is `await this.loraDeviceRepository.delete({ id })`, with no tenant term in its own predicate — and that fact is given. The obligations ask something different: is the row set that call can touch actually unconstrained by tenant, and must a person change product code or configuration to fix it. The pinned excerpt covers lines 1-291 and does not reach line 2311, so the file was read at the snapshot; its head matched the excerpt (the tenant-schema helpers imported at lines 4-6).\\n\\nPREMISE 2 FAILS — THE CALLER IS THE SCOPE. `removeLoRaDevice(edgeDeviceId, id, tenantId)` (line 2303) receives the tenant as a parameter, loads the row with the tenant IN the predicate — `findOne({ where: { id, tenantId } })` (2304-2305) — and throws `NotFoundException` (2307-2308) when that pair matches nothing. Line 2311 then deletes by the UUID primary key of the row that guard just proved belongs to the calling tenant. The sequence is check-then-act on the same server-generated primary key, so the delete's reachable row set is exactly the one row already authorised. Premise 2 denies that the predicate, a helper, RLS or the caller restricts the call; the caller restriction is present, unconditional, and five lines above the cited call.\\n\\nPREMISE 1 FAILS — THE TABLE IS PER-TENANT, ROUTED BY search_path. `lora_devices` is not a table whose rows sit side by side across tenants. The same file proves the routing model it lives under: it imports `listTenantSchemas` and `pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath` (line 6), enumerates provisioned tenant schemas (822), pins a transaction-local search_path to one schema at a time (835), and then runs an UNQUALIFIED `UPDATE edge_devices` (838) — unqualified precisely because each tenant schema holds its own copy of these device tables — with the schema name derived from the tenant id (2592). Consulted as corroboration but outside the request's cited ref set, so named here rather than cited: `apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/entities/lora-device.entity.ts:85` declares `@Entity('lora_devices')` with no `schema:` (this repository's per-tenant-table convention), `apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations/1812000000000-EncryptLoraAppKeysAtRest.ts:53-78` fans a data migration over the `sensor` source schema PLUS every provisioned `tenant_*` schema that contains `lora_devices`, and `apps/sensor-service/src/app.module.ts:180-181,537-538,588` wires the request-scoped pin (`TenantConnectionBootstrap` patches pg pool checkout; `TenantSchemaMiddleware` sets the search_path on every route). The entity's `tenant_id` column is a second discriminator layered over schema isolation, not the isolation mechanism.\\n\\nDEFECT FAILS — NOTHING MUST CHANGE HERE. Two independent mechanisms already make a cross-tenant delete unreachable at this line: physical schema routing, and the tenant-scoped guard on the same primary key. Adding `tenantId` to the delete predicate would restate an enforced check, not close a reachable write. The defect claim 'A repository call can read or change another tenant's rows' does not hold at this location, so the finding is a false positive.\\n\\nWHY THE DISTINCTION MATTERS DOWNSTREAM. This verdict is consumed by `feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus` and rolls into the adapter's precision metric and goldset. Marking a guarded delete-by-primary-key as a true positive would mint a change request that alters no behaviour and would teach the corpus that the guard-then-act shape is a defect — a shape used across sibling services in this repository, so the noise multiplies per service rather than staying local, and the same rule's earlier samples on this very file already consumed operator adjudication. The detector repair that raises precision is to widen the rule's window from the single call to the enclosing method: treat a tenant-scoped read of the same primary key, or an entity declared without `schema:` under per-request search_path pinning, as satisfying the tenant constraint. That repair belongs in the adapter's own lane, not in product code.\\n\\nCONFIDENCE. Held at 0.93: the verdict rests on two independent, directly read grounds, and the guard sits in the same method as the cited call. The residual uncertainty is the narrow reading in which a reviewer wants `tenantId` on the delete predicate as a second barrier against a future refactor that drops the guard — a hardening preference, not the stated defect, and premise 2 explicitly admits caller-side restriction.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:6\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:822\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:835\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:838\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2303\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2304\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2305\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2307\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2308\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2311\",\n        \"apps/sensor-service/src/edge-device/edge-device.service.ts:2592\"\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\n```","ttft_ms":9664,"type":"result","duration_ms":272720,"uuid":"3cee2f47-5ac8-4ff3-9a23-5c148a8e4f8c","ttft_stream_ms":2064,"time_to_request_ms":83,"first_content_frame_ms":2064,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
